try again later\") {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// CheckForkSuccess waits for github fork to complete.\n// Forks can take up to 5 minutes to complete according to GitHub.\nfunc (g *Client) CheckForkSuccess(ownerName string, forkRepoName string) bool {\n\tfor i := 0;
